Notas de Release: Concentrador 9.6.0

Información de Despliegue

Versión: 9.6.0

Fecha de Despliegue: 30/06/2026

AWS Task: [Pendiente]

JIRA Task: GITSFP-1585

Commits: 8c5b036dfe9ae06c8bda48b398a3b201e979e22c

Resumen General de la Versión

Análisis Técnico Detallado

api/src/app.js

Modificaciones Identificadas

1. Remoción de logs formateados de consola

Se elimina la función auxiliar de registro en consola para excepciones de tokens expirados o firmas inválidas.

2. Simplificación en middleware de manejo de errores

El middleware de captura de errores de JWT ahora propaga la respuesta estructurada sin llamadas redundantes a la consola estándar.

 ];
 
const createConsoleLog = (headers, res) => {
  console.log('
' + '***********************************************************************************');
  console.log(
    'Fecha: ' +
      new Date() +
      ' BranchId: ' +
      headers['branch-id'] +
      ', Respuesta: ' +
      res +
      ' Agente: ' +
      headers['agent-version'] +
      ' Notificador: ' +
      headers['notifications-version'] +
      ', Componente: ' +
      headers['component']
  );
  console.log('***********************************************************************************' + '
');
};

 const cors = require('cors');
 const https = require('https');
 const app = express();
 app.use(bodyParser.json({ limit: '50mb', extended: true }));
 app.use(bodyParser.urlencoded({ limit: '50mb', extended: true }));
// ...
 app.use(function (err, req, res, next) {
   let msg = err.message;
   if (err.message === 'jwt expired') msg = 'Expired token.';
   if (err.message === 'invalid signature') msg = 'Invalid token.';
   const custerr = new CustomError(CORE.TOKEN, msg, req.uuid);
  createConsoleLog(req.headers, 401);
   return res.status(401).json(custerr).end();
 });
 
 app.use((req, res, next) => checkRoutePermissions(req, res, next));

Conclusión del Archivo

El archivo de configuración principal de la aplicación Express simplifica el control de errores de autenticación eliminando salidas por consola obsoletas.

api/src/controllers/activeSoftware.js

Modificaciones Identificadas

1. Depuración de impresiones de diagnóstico

Se elimina la salida de consola que volcaba información del objeto de administración al procesar la versión activa de software.

Código ANTES

         actualVersion: branch.smartfran_sw.notificator.installedVersion,

Conclusión del Archivo

El controlador de software activo reduce el ruido en logs al eliminar salidas directas de consola en las funciones de parseo.

api/src/controllers/branch.js

Modificaciones Identificadas

1. Migración a cliente HTTP unificado

Se reemplazan todas las peticiones directas de axios por el cliente personalizado con interceptores de monitoreo de performance.

2. Estandarización de logs de error y depuración

Se incorporan categorías y niveles formalizados al invocar los métodos de registro de logs para todos los flujos de comunicación con las plataformas.

async function searchToken(user_id, tenantId, cached = false) { 
   let tokenCached = false;
   let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = tokensCache.get(user_id);
   if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud && !cached) {
     const dbToken = await tokenMercadoPagoModel.findOne({
       'tokenMercadoPago.user_id': user_id
     });
     tokenMercadoPagoCloud = dbToken?.tokenMercadoPago;
    if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) 
      tokensCache.set(user_id, tokenMercadoPagoCloud);    
   }
 
   if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud || cached) {
     tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await initialData.getTokenMercadoPagoCloud(
       user_id,
// ...
       mercadoPagoGetToken_uri
     );
     if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {
       tokensCache.set(user_id, tokenMercadoPagoCloud);
     }
  } else 
     tokenCached = true;
  if(!tokenMercadoPagoCloud) 
     Log.saveError(new Error('No se pudo obtener token MercadoPago Cloud'), { message: 'No token MercadoPago Cloud', user_id, tenantId });
   else tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached = tokenCached;
   return tokenMercadoPagoCloud;
 }

     } catch (error) {
      Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló loginCountryUber general', country: country, clientId: clientId, platform: 'UberEats' });
    }     
   }
 }

 async function checkOfflinePOSuber() {
   try {
     let extraData = [];
     const dateNow = new Date();
     const date3minutes = new Date(dateNow.getTime() - (1 * 60 * 1000));
  
     let branchesToClose = await model.find({
       platforms: {
         $elemMatch: {
           platform: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(uberId),
           isActive: true
// ...
           "reason": "STORE CLOSED"
         };
         const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlUber}/v1/delivery/store/${store_id}/update-store-status`;
         try {
           if (branch.address.country === "Argentina")
              await axios.post(urlAvailability, body, headersConfigAr);          
          if (branch.address.country === "Chile") 
              await axios.post(urlAvailability, body, headersConfigCl);                  
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: store_id,
             type: "Closed",
             result: true
           });
// ...

             branchReference: store_id,
             type: "Closed",
             result: false
           });
          Log.saveError(error, { 
            message: `Branch OpenClose Error: Uber checkOfflinePOSuber CLOSE ${branchId}`,

             platformId: 4,

             branch: {
                branchReferenceId: store_id,
                branchId: branchId
             },
             request: {
                url: urlAvailability,
                method: 'POST',
                body: body,
                header: branch.address.country === "Argentina" ? headersConfigAr.headers : headersConfigCl.headers
             },
             response: {
                status: error?.response?.status,
                data: error?.response?.data
             }
           });
         }
       }));
       await model.findByIdAndUpdate(branch.id, { alreadyClosedUber: true });
// ...
           "status": "ONLINE",
           "reason": "STORE OPEN"
         };

          if (branch.address.country === "Argentina") 
            await axios.post(urlAvailability, body, headersConfigAr);          
          if (branch.address.country === "Chile") 
            await axios.post(urlAvailability, body, headersConfigCl);          
 
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: store_id,
             type: "Open",
             result: true
// ...

             branchReference: store_id,
             type: "Open",
             result: false
           });
          Log.saveError(error, { 
            message: `Branch OpenClose Error: Uber checkOfflinePOSuber OPEN ${branch.branchId}`,

 async function checkOfflinePOSpeya() {
   try {
// ...
       const branchNames = Array.isArray(branchName) ? branchName : [branchName];
 
       await Promise.allSettled(branchNames.map(async (remoteId) => {
         const urlAvailability = `${peyaBaseUrl}/v2/chains/${settings.chainCode}/remoteVendors/${remoteId}/availability`;
         try {
          const statuspos = await axios.get(urlAvailability, headersConfig3);
          if(statuspos.data.length > 1) 
          {
            Log.saveError(new Error('Multiple POS status returned'), { 
              message: `Branch OpenClose Error: PedidosYa checkOfflinePOSpeya CLOSE ${branchId} - Multiple POS status returned`, 
               action: 'checkOfflinePOSpeya',
               platformId: 1,
               operation: 'CLOSE',
               branch: {
                  branchReferenceId: remoteId,
                  country: branch.address?.country,
                  branchId: branchId
               },
               data: statuspos?.data || 'No data'
             });
           }
           for (const element of statuspos.data) {
// ...
               "availabilityState": "CLOSED",
               "closedReason": "OTHER",
               "platformKey": element.platformKey,
               "platformRestaurantId": element.platformRestaurantId
             };
            try {

               branchData.results.push({
                 branchName: remoteId,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: true
               });
// ...

                 branchName: remoteId,

               });

 async function ckeckOfflinePOSmercadopago() {
   try {
// ...
       },
       lastGetNews: { $gt: dateMinuteLess },
       alreadyClosedMercadoPago: { $in: [null, true] }
     }).populate("chain");
 
    for(let tenant of tenants) {   
      let branchesToClose2 = branchesToClose.filter( r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
      let branchesToOpen2  = branchesToOpen.filter( r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
 
       if (branchesToClose2.length === 0 && branchesToOpen2.length === 0) {
         // console.log('Continue tenant', tenant.name, 'matches close', branchesToClose2.length, 'open', branchesToOpen2.length);
         continue;
       }
 
       const closePromises = branchesToClose2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
        const branchData = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
        const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];  
         const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());
         let body = {
           "status": "paused"
         };
         let user_id = mercadoPagoPlatform?.mercadoPagoUserId;
        let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId,false);
         let decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud;
         if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {
           decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
           }
          await Promise.allSettled(branchReferences.map(async (store_id) => {    
            const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;    
             try {
               try {
                await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {
                if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {                
                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId,true);
                   decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                   const headersConfig2 = {
                     headers: {
                       'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                     }
                   }
                  await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig2);
                 } else throw error;
              }           
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: true
               });
             } catch (error) {
              console.log(error);
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: false
               });
              Log.saveError(error, { 
              message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago checkOfflinePOSmercadopago CLOSE ${branchId}`, 
              action: 'checkOfflinePOSmercadopago',
              platformId: 8,
              operation: 'CLOSE',
              branch: {
                   branchReferenceId: store_id,
                   user_id: user_id,
                   branchId: branchId
              },
              request: {
                   url: urlAvailability,
                   method: 'POST',
                   body: body,
                   header: headersConfig
              },
              response: {
                   status: error?.response?.status,
                   data: error?.response?.data
              }
               });
    
             }
           }));
           await model.findByIdAndUpdate(branch.id, { alreadyClosedMercadoPago: true });
        
         } else {
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: store_id,
             type: "Closed",
             result: false
           });
         }
        extraData.push(branchData);     
       });
  
       const openPromises = branchesToOpen2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
         const branchData = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
         const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
        const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());    
         let body = {
           "status": "enabled"
         };
         let user_id = mercadoPagoPlatform?.mercadoPagoUserId;
        let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, false);

           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
           }
          await Promise.allSettled(branchReferences.map(async (store_id) => {  
             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;
            try {                
               try {
                await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {
                if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {                
                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud =  await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId,true);
                   decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                   const headersConfig2 = {
                     headers: {
                       'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                     }
                   }
                  await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig2);
                }  else throw error;
               }
               branchData.results.push({

                 result: true
// ...

 async function sendStateMercadoPago(country) {
   try {
     let extraData = [];
    
     // Branches to Close
     let branchesToClose = await model.find({
       platforms: {
         $elemMatch: {
           platform: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(mercadopagoId),
// ...
       alreadyClosedMercadoPago: false
     }).populate('chain');
 
 
     // State Branches
    for ( let tenant of tenants){
      let branchesToClose2 = branchesToClose.filter( r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
      let branchesToOpen2  = branchesToOpen.filter( r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
 
       if (branchesToClose2.length === 0 && branchesToOpen2.length === 0) {
         // console.log('Continue tenant', tenant.name, 'matches close', branchesToClose2.length, 'open', branchesToOpen2.length);
         continue;
      }     
 
       // Promises to send state branches MercadoPago
       const closePromises = branchesToClose2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
         const branchData = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
        const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];  
         const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());
 
         let body = {
           "status": "paused"
         };
// ...
           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
           }
          await Promise.allSettled(branchReferences.map(async (store_id) => {    
            const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;    
             try {
               try {
                await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {
                 // significa que dio error al obtener token y token no está en memoria intenta traer token desde cloud
                if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {                
                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId,true);
                   decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                   const headersConfig2 = {
                     headers: {
                       'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                     }
                   }
                  await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig2);
                 } else throw error;
              }           
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: true
               });
             } catch (error) {
              console.log(error);
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: false
               });
              Log.saveError(error, { 
              message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendStatusMercadoPago CLOSE ${branchId}`, 
              action: 'sendStatusMercadoPago',
              platformId: 8,
              operation: 'CLOSE',
              branch: {
                   branchReferenceId: store_id,
                   user_id: user_id,
                   branchId: branchId
              },
              request: {
                   url: urlAvailability,
                   method: 'PUT',
                   body: body,
                   header: headersConfig
              },
              response: {
                   status: error?.response?.status,
                   data: error?.response?.data
              }
               });
    
             }
           }));
         } else {
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: branchId,
             type: "Closed",
             result: false
           });
         }
        extraData.push(branchData);     
       });
  
       const openPromises = branchesToOpen2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
         const branchData = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
         const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
        const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());    
         let body = {
           "status": "enabled"
         };
         let user_id = mercadoPagoPlatform?.mercadoPagoUserId;
        let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, false);  
         let decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud;
         if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {
           decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
           }
          await Promise.allSettled(branchReferences.map(async (store_id) => {  
             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;
            try {                
               try {
                await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {
                if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {                
                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud =  await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId,true);
                   decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                   const headersConfig2 = {
                     headers: {
                       'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                     }
                   }
                  await axios.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig2);
                }  else throw error;
               }
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Open",
                 result: true
// ...
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Open",
                 result: false
               });
              Log.saveError(error, { 
              message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendStatusMercadoPago OPEN ${branchId}`, 
              action: 'sendStatusMercadoPago',
              platformId: 8,
              operation: 'OPEN',
              branch: {
                   branchReferenceId: store_id,
                   user_id: user_id,
                   branchId: branchId
              },
              request: {
                   url: urlAvailability,
                   method: 'PUT',
                   body: body,
                   header: headersConfig
              },
              response: {
                   status: error?.response?.status,
                   data: error?.response?.data
              }
               });
             }
           }));
         } else {
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: branchId,
             type: "Open",
             result: false
           });
        }       
         extraData.push(branchData);
       });
 
       await Promise.allSettled([...closePromises, ...openPromises]);
       if (extraData.length > 0)
      await openClosedLogModel.create({
        result: !extraData.some(branch =>
          branch.results.some(r => r.result === false)
        ),
        Ident: "MercadoPagoCron",
        extraData,
        platformId: 8,
        type: "CRON"
      });
     }
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { 
      message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendStateMercadoPago GENERAL`, 
       action: 'sendStateMercadoPago',
       platformId: 8,
       operation: 'GENERAL'
     });
   }
 }

 async function closeBranchPeya(branch, platform) {
   try {
     const branchName = platform.branchName;
     const branchNames = Array.isArray(branchName) ? branchName : [branchName];
    
     const headersConfig = {
       headers: {
         'Authorization': `Bearer ${tokenPeya}`,
         'Content-Type': 'application/json'
       }
     };
    
     for (const remoteId of branchNames) {
       const url = `${peyaBaseUrl}/v2/chains/${settings.chainCode}/remoteVendors/${remoteId}/availability`;
      const statusResponse = await axios.get(url, headersConfig);
       for (const element of statusResponse.data) {
         const body = {
           availabilityState: "CLOSED",
           closedReason: "OTHER",
           platformKey: element.platformKey,
           platformRestaurantId: element.platformRestaurantId
         };
         try {
          await axios.put(url, body, headersConfig);
         } catch (error) {
          Log.saveError(error, { 
            message: `Branch OpenClose Error: PedidosYa sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed CLOSE ${branch.branchId}`, 
             action: 'sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed',
             platformId: 1, // peyaId
             operation: 'CLOSE',
             branch: {
                branchReferenceId: remoteId,
                country: branch.address?.country,
                branchId: branch.branchId
             },
             request: {
                url: url,
                method: 'PUT',
                body: body,
                header: headersConfig.headers
             },
             response: {
                status: error?.response?.status,
                data: error?.response?.data
             },
             element: element
           });
         }
       }
      console.log(`Cerrado en PedidosYa: branch ${branch.branchId}, remoteId ${remoteId}`);
     }
   } catch (error) {
     throw error;
   }
 }

 async function closeBranchRappi(branch, platform) {
   try {
     const branchReference = platform.branchReference;
     const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
     const country = branch.address?.country;
    
     if (!tokenRappi[country]) {
       throw new Error(`No hay token de Rappi para ${country}`);
     }
 
     const url = baseUrlRappi[country] + '/api/v2/restaurants-integrations-public-api/availability/stores/enable';
// ...
       }
     };
 
     for (const rappiNumber of branchReferences) {
       try {
        await axios.put(url, { stores: [{ store_id: rappiNumber, is_enabled: false }] }, options);
       } catch (error) {
        Log.saveError(error, { 
          message: `Branch OpenClose Error: Rappi sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed CLOSE ${branch.branchId}`, 
           action: 'sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed',
           platformId: 2, // rappiId
           operation: 'CLOSE',
           branch: {
              branchReferenceId: rappiNumber,
              country: branch.address?.country,
              branchId: branch.branchId
           },
           request: {
              url: url,
              method: 'PUT',
              body: { stores: [{ store_id: rappiNumber, is_enabled: false }] },
              header: options.headers
           },
           response: {
              status: error?.response?.status,
              data: error?.response?.data
           }
         });
       }
   
      console.log(`Cerrado en Rappi: branch ${branch.branchId}, store_id ${rappiNumber}`);
     }
   } catch (error) {
     throw error;
   }
 }

     const user_id = mercadoPagoPlatform?.mercadoPagoUserId;
     const tenant = tenants.find(t => t.name === branch.chain?.trim().toLowerCase());
    
     if (!user_id || !tenant) {
       throw new Error(`No se encontró user_id o tenant para branch ${branch.branchId}`);

     if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {

     }
 
     const decryptedToken = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);

        console.log(`Cerrado en MercadoPago: branch ${branch.branchId}, store_id ${store_id}`);
       } catch (error) {
        Log.saveError(error, { 
          message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed CLOSE ${branch.branchId}`, 
           action: 'sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed',

         if (resp?.data?.horarios?.takeaway?.mensaje) {
           Log.saveError(null, {
            message: 'Falló GET getInfoBranchesGrido()',
            mensaje: resp.data.horarios.takeaway.mensaje, urlAvailability, headersConfig,
             platform: 'PediGrido-Concentrador',
           })
           return null;
         }
 
         return { branch, data: resp.data };
       } catch (error) {
         Log.saveError(error, {
          message: 'Falló GET getInfoBranchesGrido()',
           platform: 'PediGrido-Concentrador'
         });
         return null;
       }
     });

     }
 
     // Detiene, elimina y crea nuevamente CRONS de horarios branches Grido
     cronHorariosPediGrido();

    console.log(error)
     Log.saveError(error, {
      message: `Falló getInfoBranchesGrido PediGrido`,
       platform: 'PediGrido-Concentrador'
     });
   }
 }

 async function sendReject(orderInfo, internalCode) {
  try {       
     switch (internalCode) {
       case 1: { //'PEDIDOSYA'
         let fullOrder = await orderModel.findOne({
           'order.code': orderInfo.order.id
         });
// ...
         const bodyReject = {
           message: 'Orden rechazada por local cerrado API',
           reason: 'CLOSED',
           status: "order_rejected"
         };
     
         try {
          await axios.post(fullOrder.order.callbackUrls.orderRejectedUrl, bodyReject, headersConfig3);
         } catch (error) {
          console.log(error)
          Log.saveError(error, {                     
            message: 'Platform Communication Error: PedidosYa branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,                    
            action: 'branchRejectOrder', 
            platformId: 1, 
             order: {
              "id": orderInfo.order.id,             
              "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),     
               "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
              "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,              
               "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
            }, 
            request: { 
               url: fullOrder.order.callbackUrls.orderRejectedUrl,
               body: bodyReject,
               header: headersConfig3
             }
           });
         }
   
         break;
       }
 
       case 2: { //'RAPPI'    
         let url = null, data = null, options = null;
// ...
 
           data = {
             reason: "STORE CLOSED"
           };
           try {
            await axios.put(url, data, options);
           } catch (error) {
            console.log(error)
            Log.saveError(error, {                     
              message: 'Platform Communication Error: Rappi branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,                    
              action: 'branchRejectOrder', 
              platformId: 2, 
               order: {
                "id": orderInfo.order.id,             
                "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),     
                 "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
                "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,              
                 "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
              }, 
              request: { 
                 url: url,
                 body: data,
                 header: options
               }
             });
          }     
         } catch (errorRappi) {
          Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló reject order concentrador rappi', platform: 'Rappi-Concentrador', tokenRappi: tokenRappi, orderInfo: orderInfo, url: url, data: data, options: options });
         }
         break;
       }
 
       case 4: { //'UBEREATS'
// ...
           }
         };
 
         const url = `${baseUrlUber}/v1/delivery/order/${fullOrder.order.oldId}/cancel`;
         try {
          await axios.post(url, body, headersConfig);
         } catch (error) {
          console.log(error)

       }

       case 8: {

         }
        let tenant = tenants.find(t => t.name ===  orderInfo.extraData.chain.trim().toLowerCase());

         });

             headers: {

             }

              await axios.put(url, body, headersConfig);

                 decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);

                   headers: {
                     'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                   }
                 }

              }  else throw error;
             }

            Log.saveError(error, {                     
              message: 'Platform Communication Error: MercadoPago branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,

              platformId: 8,

               }

        }   
       }

     }
   } catch (error) {

   }
 }

          await axios.put(urlAddWebhook, bodyAddStores, options);
        }     
       }
       else { // estaba activa -> eliminar del webhook    
         let oldRappi = currentBranch.platforms.find(r => r.platform.toString() === 'f3fc2fb96e42d11cc7c65b4b'); // Buscar la versión vieja  
         if (oldRappi?.branchReference) { // Solo eliminar si el viejo tenía branchReference
           let bodyDeleteStores = {
             "stores": Array.isArray(oldRappi.branchReference) ? oldRappi.branchReference : [oldRappi.branchReference]
           };
 
          await axios.delete(urlDeleteWebhook, {
             data: bodyDeleteStores,
             ...options
           });
         }
       }
     }
     return res.status(200).send(errors).end();
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló al guardar branch concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });
     error = error.toString();
     logger.error({
       message: 'Failed to update branch.',

// ...

         return res.status(400).json({ error: msg }).end();
       }
     })
     .catch((e) => {
      console.log(e);
     });
 };

const createConsoleLog = (headers, res) => {
  console.log('
' + '***********************************************************************************');
  console.log(
    'Fecha: ' +
    new Date() +
    ' BranchId: ' +
    headers['branch-id'] +
    ', Respuesta: ' +
    res +
    ' Agente: ' +
    headers['agent-version'] +
    ' Notificador: ' +
    headers['notifications-version'] +
    ', Componente: ' +
    headers['component']
  );
  console.log('***********************************************************************************' + '
');
};

 const login = async (req, res) => {
   try {
     if (!req.body || !req.body.branchId || !req.body.branchSecret) {
      createConsoleLog(req.headers, 400);
       logger.error({ message: 'Insufficient params.', meta: req.body });
       return res
         .status(400)
         .json({
           error: 'Insufficient params.'
// ...
         await syncCheckUTC();
 
 
       }
     } catch (error) {
      console.log(error);
      Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló cronAlready concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });
     }
 
     let savedBranch;
     if (req.headers.updaterversion)
       savedBranch = await model
// ...
         aws_id: savedBranch.credentials.aws_id,
         aws_secret: savedBranch.credentials.aws_secret,
         branch_queue: config.AWS.SQS.ORDER_CONSUMER.NAME,
         platform_queue: config.AWS.SQS.ORDER_PRODUCER.NAME.replace('##_', savedBranch.branchId)
       };
      createConsoleLog(req.headers, 200);
       return res
         .status(200)
         .json({
           data: user,
           accessToken
// ...
     return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Login failed.' }).end();
   }
 };
 
 const loginCloud = async (req, res) => {
  let params ={ 
     user: req.body.user,
    password :req.body.password
   }
   let findParams = {};
   findParams.permissions = true;
  let user = await userModel.findOne({user: params.user}, {salt: true, password: true  }); 
  if (!user) 
    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Username or password are incorrect.' }).end(); 
  if(user.salt)
    params.password = sha256(params.password + user.salt).toString(); 
  if (!user.salt){ 
     const randomBytes = require('crypto').randomBytes;
     const salt = randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
     const saltedPassword = user.password + salt;
     const password = sha256(saltedPassword).toString();
    await userModel.updateOne({user: params.user}, {password: password, passwordConfirm: password, salt: salt });    
     params.password = sha256(params.password + salt).toString();
   }
  try { 
    let user = await userModel.findOne(params, findParams); 
     if (!!user) {
      const accessToken = jwt.sign({ user }, settings.token.secret, {  expiresIn: '24h' });
       return res.status(200).json({ data: user, accessToken }).end();
    } 
     else
      throw 'Username or password are incorrect.';    
  } catch (error) { 
     Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló loginCloud concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });
     return res.status(401).json({ error: error }).end();
   }
 };
 
 
 const getBranchesToExport = async (req, res) => {
  try {   
     const { chain, country, page, size } = req.body;
     let matchStage = { deletedAt: null };
     if (country?.length) matchStage['address.country'] = { $in: country };
     if (chain?.length) matchStage.chain = { $in: chain };
     const branchesToExport = await model.find(matchStage)
       .select('branchId platforms chain address.country name')
       .sort({ branchId: 1 })
       .skip(page * size)
       .limit(size)
       .lean();
      return res.status(200).json(branchesToExport).end();
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló getBranchesToExport concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });
   }
 };
 
 const getBranchesToExportCount = async (req, res) => {
  try {   
     const { chain, country } = req.body;
     let matchStage = { deletedAt: null };
     if (country?.length) matchStage['address.country'] = { $in: country };
     if (chain?.length) matchStage.chain = { $in: chain };
    const branchesToExport = await model.countDocuments(matchStage); 
      return res.status(200).json(branchesToExport).end();
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló getBranchesToExport concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });
   }
 };

 const getStatus = async (req, res) => {//RESPONDER EN sendOpen
// ...
           const branchName = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == peyaId).branchName;
           const branchNames = Array.isArray(branchName) ? branchName : [branchName];
           for (const remoteId of branchNames) {
             const urlAvailability = `${peyaBaseUrl}/v2/chains/${settings.chainCode}/remoteVendors/${remoteId}/availability`;
             try {
              const statuspos = await axios.get(urlAvailability, headersConfig3);
               //retorna el primer estado
               if (statuspos.data[0].availabilityState === "CLOSED") {
                 result.push({ platform: 1, closed: true })
                 continue;
               }
// ...
                 continue;
               }
               throw Error("Estado no existe")
             } catch (error) {
               result.push({ platform: 1, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
              Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló ', platform: 'PedidosYa-Concentrador', urlAvailability: urlAvailability, body: req.body, headersConfig3: headersConfig3 });
 
             }
           }
         }
 
// ...
           const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == uberId).branchReference;
           const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
           for (const store_id of branchReferences) {
             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlUber}/v1/delivery/store/${store_id}/status`;
             try {
              if(branch.address.country === "Argentina"){
                let statuspos = await axios.get(urlAvailability, headersConfigAr);
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "OFFLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "ONLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: false })
                   continue;
                }    
               }
              if(branch.address.country === "Chile"){
                let statuspos = await axios.get(urlAvailability, headersConfigCl);
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "OFFLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "ONLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: false })
                   continue;
                }     
               }
                   
               throw Error("Estado no existe")
 
             } catch (error) {
               result.push({ platform: 4, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
              Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló obtener estado uber BO', platform: 'UberEats-Concentrador', urlAvailability: urlAvailability, body: req.body, headersConfigCl: headersConfigCl, headersConfigAr: headersConfigAr });
             }
           }
         }
 
         // rappi
// ...
           const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == rappiId).branchReference;
           const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
           for (const rappiNumber of branchReferences) {
             if (branch.address.country === "Argentina") {
               try {
                let status = await axios.post(urlAr, [rappiNumber], optionsAr);
                 if (status.data[rappiNumber] === false) {
                   result.push({ platform: 2, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: false })
               } catch (error) {
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
                Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló obtener estado rappi BO', platform: 'Concentrador', urlAr: urlAr, rappiNumber: rappiNumber, optionsAr: optionsAr });
               }
             }
             if (branch.address.country === "Perú") {
               try {
                let status = await axios.post(urlPr, [rappiNumber], optionsPr);
                 if (status.data[rappiNumber] === false) {
                   result.push({ platform: 2, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: false })
               } catch (error) {
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
                Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló obtener estado rappi BO', platform: 'Concentrador', urlPr: urlPr, rappiNumber: rappiNumber, optionsPr: optionsPr });
               }
             }
             if (branch.address.country === "Chile") {
               try {
                let status = await axios.post(urlCl, [rappiNumber], optionsCl);
                 if (status.data[rappiNumber] === false) {
                   result.push({ platform: 2, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: false })
               } catch (error) {
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
                Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló obtener estado rappi BO', platform: 'Concentrador', urlCl: urlCl, rappiNumber: rappiNumber, optionsCl: optionsCl });
               }
             }
             if (branch.address.country === "Uruguay") {
               try {
                let status = await axios.post(urlUr, [rappiNumber], optionsUr);
                 if (status.data[rappiNumber] === false) {
                   result.push({ platform: 2, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: false })
               } catch (error) {
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
                Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló obtener estado rappi BO', platform: 'Concentrador', urlUr: urlUr, rappiNumber: rappiNumber, optionsUr: optionsUr });
               }
             }
           }
         }
 
// ...
           const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == pedigridoId).branchReference;
           const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
           for (const store_id of branchReferences) {
             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlPedigrido}v1/locals/horario?branch=${store_id}`;
             try {
              const statuspos = await axios.get(urlAvailability, headersConfigPediGrido);
               if (statuspos.data.estado === "apagado") {
                 result.push({ platform: 7, closed: true })
                 continue;
               }
               if (statuspos.data.estado === "encendido") {
                 result.push({ platform: 7, closed: false })
                 continue;
               }
               throw Error("Estado no existe")
             } catch (error) {
              console.log('error')
              console.log(error)
               result.push({ platform: 7, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
              Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló obtener estado pedigrido BO', platform: 'PediGrido-Concentrador', urlAvailability: urlAvailability, body: req.body, headersConfig: headersConfigPediGrido });
             }
           }
         }
 
         // mercadoPago
         if (branchForm.platform === 8) {
           try {
             const platformTemp = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId);
             const branchReference = platformTemp?.branchReference;
            const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];     
            let user_id = platformTemp?.mercadoPagoUserId;    
            let tenant = tenants.find(r => r.name === branch.chain.chain.toLowerCase());     
             let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, false);
             let decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
             const headersConfig = {
               headers: {
                 'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
// ...
             for (const store_id of branchReferences) {
               const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}`;
               try {
                 let statuspos;
                 try {
                  statuspos = await axios.get(urlAvailability, headersConfig);
                 } catch (error) {
                  if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {                
                    tokenMercadoPagoCloud =  await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId,true);
                     decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                     const headersConfig2 = {
                       headers: {
                         'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                       }
                     }
                    statuspos = await axios.get(urlAvailability, headersConfig2);
                   }
                   else throw error;
                 }
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "DISABLED" || statuspos.data.status === "PAUSED") {
                   result.push({ platform: 8, closed: true })
// ...
                   result.push({ platform: 8, closed: false })
                   continue;
                 }
                 throw Error("Estado no existe")
               } catch (error) {
                console.log('error')
                console.log(error)
                 result.push({ platform: 8, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
                Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló obtener estado MercadoPago BO',branchId: branch.branchId, user_id: user_id, platform: 'MercadoPago-Concentrador', urlAvailability: urlAvailability, body: req.body, headersConfig: headersConfig });
               }
             }
           } catch (error) {
             result.push({ platform: 8, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
            console.log('error')
            console.log(error);
            Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló General estado MercadoPago BO', platform: 'MercadoPago-Concentrador', body: req.body, headersConfig: req.headers });
          }          
         }
       } catch (error) {
        Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló getStatus concentrador IN', branchForm: branchForm, platform: 'Concentrador' });
         continue;
       }
     }
     return res.status(200).json(result).end();
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló getStatus concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });
     return res.status(200).json(error.message).end();
   }
 };

               }
            });              
           }
         }
       }
     }
     return res.status(200).json({}).end();

    Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló sendOpen concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });

async function searchToken(user_id, tenantId, cached = false) {
   let tokenCached = false;
   let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = tokensCache.get(user_id);
   if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud && !cached) {
     const dbToken = await tokenMercadoPagoModel.findOne({
       'tokenMercadoPago.user_id': user_id
     });
     tokenMercadoPagoCloud = dbToken?.tokenMercadoPago;
    if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud)
      tokensCache.set(user_id, tokenMercadoPagoCloud);
   }
 
   if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud || cached) {
     tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await initialData.getTokenMercadoPagoCloud(
       user_id,
// ...
       mercadoPagoGetToken_uri
     );
     if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {
       tokensCache.set(user_id, tokenMercadoPagoCloud);
     }
  } else
     tokenCached = true;
  if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud)
     Log.saveError(new Error('No se pudo obtener token MercadoPago Cloud'), { message: 'No token MercadoPago Cloud', user_id, tenantId });
   else tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached = tokenCached;
   return tokenMercadoPagoCloud;
 }

 async function ckeckOfflinePOSmercadopago() {
   try {
// ...
       },
       lastGetNews: { $gt: dateMinuteLess },
       alreadyClosedMercadoPago: { $in: [null, true] }
     }).populate("chain");
 
    for (let tenant of tenants) {
      let branchesToClose2 = branchesToClose.filter(r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
      let branchesToOpen2 = branchesToOpen.filter(r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
 
       if (branchesToClose2.length === 0 && branchesToOpen2.length === 0) {
         // console.log('Continue tenant', tenant.name, 'matches close', branchesToClose2.length, 'open', branchesToOpen2.length);
         continue;
       }
 
       const closePromises = branchesToClose2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
        const branchDSata = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
        const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
         const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());
         let body = {
           "status": "paused"
         };
         let user_id = mercadoPagoPlatform?.mercadoPagoUserId;
        let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, false);
         let decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud;
         if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {
           decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
           }
          await Promise.allSettled(branchReferences.map(async (store_id) => {
            const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;
             try {
               try {
                await httpClient.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {
                if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {
                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, true);
                   decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                   const headersConfig2 = {
                     headers: {
                       'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                     }
                   }
                  await httpClient.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig2);
                 } else throw error;
              }
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: true
               });
             } catch (error) {
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: false
               });
              Log.saveError(error, {
                message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago checkOfflinePOSmercadopago CLOSE ${branchId}`,
                action: 'checkOfflinePOSmercadopago',
                platformId: 8,
                operation: 'CLOSE',
                branch: {
                   branchReferenceId: store_id,
                   user_id: user_id,
                   branchId: branchId
                },
                request: {
                   url: urlAvailability,
                   method: 'POST',
                   body: body,
                   header: headersConfig
                },
                response: {
                   status: error?.response?.status,
                   data: error?.response?.data
                }
               });

             }
           }));
           await model.findByIdAndUpdate(branch.id, { alreadyClosedMercadoPago: true });

         } else {
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: store_id,
             type: "Closed",
             result: false
           });
         }
        extraData.push(branchData);
       });

       const openPromises = branchesToOpen2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
         const branchData = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
         const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
        const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());
         let body = {
           "status": "enabled"
         };

             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;

               try {
                await httpClient.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {

                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, true);

         }
       }));
       extraData.push(branchData);
// ...
       extraData,
       platformId: 2,
       type: "CRON"
     });
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, {
      message: `Branch OpenClose Error: Rappi enviarEstadoRappi GENERAL_CRON`,
       action: 'enviarEstadoRappi',
       platformId: 2,
       operation: 'GENERAL'
     });
   }
 }
 
 async function sendStateMercadoPago(country) {
   try {
     let extraData = [];

     // Branches to Close
     let branchesToClose = await model.find({
       platforms: {
         $elemMatch: {
           platform: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(mercadopagoId),
// ...
       alreadyClosedMercadoPago: false
     }).populate('chain');
 
 
     // State Branches
    for (let tenant of tenants) {
      let branchesToClose2 = branchesToClose.filter(r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
      let branchesToOpen2 = branchesToOpen.filter(r => r.chain?.chain.trim().toLowerCase() === tenant.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase());
 
       if (branchesToClose2.length === 0 && branchesToOpen2.length === 0) {
         // console.log('Continue tenant', tenant.name, 'matches close', branchesToClose2.length, 'open', branchesToOpen2.length);
         continue;
      }
 
       // Promises to send state branches MercadoPago
       const closePromises = branchesToClose2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
         const branchData = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
        const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
         const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());
 
         let body = {
           "status": "paused"
         };
// ...
           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
           }
          await Promise.allSettled(branchReferences.map(async (store_id) => {
            const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;
             try {
               try {
                await httpClient.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {
                 // significa que dio error al obtener token y token no está en memoria intenta traer token desde cloud
                if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {
                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, true);
                   decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                   const headersConfig2 = {
                     headers: {
                       'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                     }
                   }
                  await httpClient.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig2);
                 } else throw error;
              }
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: true
               });
             } catch (error) {
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Closed",
                 result: false
               });
              Log.saveError(error, {
                message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendStatusMercadoPago CLOSE ${branchId}`,
                action: 'sendStatusMercadoPago',
                platformId: 8,
                operation: 'CLOSE',
                branch: {
                   branchReferenceId: store_id,
                   user_id: user_id,
                   branchId: branchId
                },
                request: {
                   url: urlAvailability,
                   method: 'PUT',
                   body: body,
                   header: headersConfig
                },
                response: {
                   status: error?.response?.status,
                   data: error?.response?.data
                }
               });

             }
           }));
         } else {
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: branchId,
             type: "Closed",
             result: false
           });
         }
        extraData.push(branchData);
       });

       const openPromises = branchesToOpen2.map(async (branch) => {
         const branchId = branch.branchId;
         const branchData = { branchId, results: [] };
         const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId).branchReference;
         const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
        const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());
         let body = {
           "status": "enabled"
         };
         let user_id = mercadoPagoPlatform?.mercadoPagoUserId;
        let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, false);
         let decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud;
         if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {
           decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
           }
          await Promise.allSettled(branchReferences.map(async (store_id) => {
             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/stores/${store_id}/status`;
            try {
               try {
                await httpClient.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig);
               } catch (error) {
                if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {
                  tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, true);
                   decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                   const headersConfig2 = {
                     headers: {
                       'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                     }
                   }
                  await httpClient.put(urlAvailability, body, headersConfig2);
                } else throw error;
               }
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Open",
                 result: true
// ...
               branchData.results.push({
                 branchReference: store_id,
                 type: "Open",
                 result: false
               });
              Log.saveError(error, {
                message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendStatusMercadoPago OPEN ${branchId}`,
                action: 'sendStatusMercadoPago',
                platformId: 8,
                operation: 'OPEN',
                branch: {
                   branchReferenceId: store_id,
                   user_id: user_id,
                   branchId: branchId
                },
                request: {
                   url: urlAvailability,
                   method: 'PUT',
                   body: body,
                   header: headersConfig
                },
                response: {
                   status: error?.response?.status,
                   data: error?.response?.data
                }
               });
             }
           }));
         } else {
           branchData.results.push({
             branchReference: branchId,
             type: "Open",
             result: false
           });
        }
         extraData.push(branchData);
       });
 
       await Promise.allSettled([...closePromises, ...openPromises]);
       if (extraData.length > 0)
        await openClosedLogModel.create({
          result: !extraData.some(branch =>
            branch.results.some(r => r.result === false)
          ),
          Ident: "MercadoPagoCron",
          extraData,
          platformId: 8,
          type: "CRON"
        });
     }
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, {
      message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendStateMercadoPago GENERAL`,
       action: 'sendStateMercadoPago',
       platformId: 8,
       operation: 'GENERAL'
     });
   }
 }

 async function closeBranchPeya(branch, platform) {
   try {
     const branchName = platform.branchName;
     const branchNames = Array.isArray(branchName) ? branchName : [branchName];

     const headersConfig = {
       headers: {
         'Authorization': `Bearer ${tokenPeya}`,
         'Content-Type': 'application/json'
       }
     };

     for (const remoteId of branchNames) {
       const url = `${peyaBaseUrl}/v2/chains/${settings.chainCode}/remoteVendors/${remoteId}/availability`;
      const statusResponse = await httpClient.get(url, headersConfig);
       for (const element of statusResponse.data) {
         const body = {
           availabilityState: "CLOSED",
           closedReason: "OTHER",
           platformKey: element.platformKey,
           platformRestaurantId: element.platformRestaurantId
         };
         try {
          await httpClient.put(url, body, headersConfig);
         } catch (error) {
          Log.saveError(error, {
            message: `Branch OpenClose Error: PedidosYa sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed CLOSE ${branch.branchId}`,
             action: 'sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed',
             platformId: 1, // peyaId
             operation: 'CLOSE',
             branch: {
              branchReferenceId: remoteId,
              country: branch.address?.country,
              branchId: branch.branchId
             },
             request: {
              url: url,
              method: 'PUT',
              body: body,
              header: headersConfig.headers
             },
             response: {
              status: error?.response?.status,
              data: error?.response?.data
             },
             element: element
           });
         }
       }
     }
   } catch (error) {
     throw error;
   }
 }

 async function closeBranchRappi(branch, platform) {
   try {
     const branchReference = platform.branchReference;
     const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
     const country = branch.address?.country;

     if (!tokenRappi[country]) {
       throw new Error(`No hay token de Rappi para ${country}`);
     }
 
     const url = baseUrlRappi[country] + '/api/v2/restaurants-integrations-public-api/availability/stores/enable';
// ...
       }
     };
 
     for (const rappiNumber of branchReferences) {
       try {
        await httpClient.put(url, { stores: [{ store_id: rappiNumber, is_enabled: false }] }, options);
       } catch (error) {
        Log.saveError(error, {
          message: `Branch OpenClose Error: Rappi sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed CLOSE ${branch.branchId}`,
           action: 'sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed',
           platformId: 2, // rappiId
           operation: 'CLOSE',
           branch: {
            branchReferenceId: rappiNumber,
            country: branch.address?.country,
            branchId: branch.branchId
           },
           request: {
            url: url,
            method: 'PUT',
            body: { stores: [{ store_id: rappiNumber, is_enabled: false }] },
            header: options.headers
           },
           response: {
            status: error?.response?.status,
            data: error?.response?.data
           }
         });
       }

     }
   } catch (error) {
     throw error;
   }
 }

 async function closeBranchMercadoPago(branch, platform) {
   try {
     const branchReference = platform.branchReference;
     const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];

     const mercadoPagoPlatform = branch.platforms?.find(p => p.platform?.toString() === mercadopagoId?.toString());

       throw new Error(`No se encontró user_id o tenant para branch ${branch.branchId}`);

     if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {

     const decryptedToken = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);

       const body = { status: "paused" };
       const url = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}users/${user_id}/stores/${store_id}`;

       try {
        await httpClient.put(url, body, headersConfig);
       } catch (error) {
        Log.saveError(error, {
          message: `Branch OpenClose Error: MercadoPago sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed CLOSE ${branch.branchId}`,
           action: 'sendClosedToPlatformsAfterRejectClosed',
           platformId: 8, // mercadopagoId
           operation: 'CLOSE',
           branch: {
            branchReferenceId: store_id,
            country: branch.address?.country,
            branchId: branch.branchId
           },
           request: {
            url: url,
            method: 'PUT',
            body: body,
            header: headersConfig.headers
           },
           response: {
            status: error?.response?.status,
            data: error?.response?.data
           }
         });
       }
     }
   } catch (error) {

 async function sendReject(orderInfo, internalCode) {
  try {
     switch (internalCode) {
       case 1: { //'PEDIDOSYA'
         let fullOrder = await orderModel.findOne({
           'order.code': orderInfo.order.id
         });
// ...
         const bodyReject = {
           message: 'Orden rechazada por local cerrado API',
           reason: 'CLOSED',
           status: "order_rejected"
         };

         try {
          await httpClient.post(fullOrder.order.callbackUrls.orderRejectedUrl, bodyReject, headersConfig3);
         } catch (error) {
          Log.saveError(error, {
            message: 'Platform Communication Error: PedidosYa branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,
            action: 'branchRejectOrder',
            platformId: 1,
             order: {
              "id": orderInfo.order.id,
              "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),
               "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
              "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,
               "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
            },
            request: {
               url: fullOrder.order.callbackUrls.orderRejectedUrl,
               body: bodyReject,
               header: headersConfig3
             }
           });
         }

         break;
       }
 
       case 2: { //'RAPPI'    
         let url = null, data = null, options = null;
// ...
 
           data = {
             reason: "STORE CLOSED"
           };
           try {
            await httpClient.put(url, data, options);
           } catch (error) {
            Log.saveError(error, {
              message: 'Platform Communication Error: Rappi branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,
              action: 'branchRejectOrder',
              platformId: 2,
               order: {
                "id": orderInfo.order.id,
                "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),
                 "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
                "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,
                 "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
              },
              request: {
                 url: url,
                 body: data,
                 header: options
               }
             });
          }
         } catch (errorRappi) {
          Log.saveError(error, { category: 'ORDER', message: '[ORDER/ERROR] rappi_reject_order failed', platform: 'Rappi-Concentrador', url: url, order_id: orderInfo?.id ?? 'unknown' });
         }
         break;
       }
 
       case 4: { //'UBEREATS'
// ...
           }
         };
 
         const url = `${baseUrlUber}/v1/delivery/order/${fullOrder.order.oldId}/cancel`;
         try {
          await httpClient.post(url, body, headersConfig);
         } catch (error) {
          Log.saveError(error, {
            message: 'Platform Communication Error: UberEats branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,
            action: 'branchRejectOrder',
            platformId: 4,
             order: {
              "id": orderInfo.order.id,
              "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),
               "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
              "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,
               "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
            },
            request: {
               url: url,
               body: body,
               header: headersConfig
             }
           });
         }

         break;
       }
 
       case 7: { //'RAPIBOY'
         const body = {
// ...
         const headers = {
           'Content-Type': 'application/json'
         };
         const url = `${baseUrlPedigrido}v1/Grido/CancelarPedido`;
         try {
          await httpClient.put(url, body, headers);
         } catch (error) {
          Log.saveError(error, {
            message: 'Platform Communication Error: PediGrido branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,
            action: 'branchRejectOrder',
            platformId: 7,
             order: {
              "id": orderInfo.order.id,
              "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),
               "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
              "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,
               "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
            },
            request: {
               url: url,
               body: body,
               header: headers
             }
           });
        }
         break;
       }
 
       //MECADO PAGO
       case 8: {
         const body = {
           status: "cancelled"
         }
        let tenant = tenants.find(t => t.name === orderInfo.extraData.chain.trim().toLowerCase());
         let fullorder = await orderModel.findOne({
           'originalId': orderInfo.order.originalId
         });
         let user_id = fullorder.user_id;

         let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, false);
        let decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud;
         if (tokenMercadoPagoCloud) {
           decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
           const headersConfig = {
             headers: {
               'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
             }
          }
           const url = `${baseUrlMercadoPago}/proximity-integration/shipments/${orderInfo.order.id}/cancel`;
           try {
             try {
              await httpClient.put(url, body, headersConfig);
             } catch (error) {
              if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {
                tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, true);
                 decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                 const headersConfig2 = {
                   headers: {
                     'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                   }
                 }
                await httpClient.put(url, body, headersConfig2);
              } else throw error;
             }
           } catch (error) {
            Log.saveError(error, {
              message: 'Platform Communication Error: MercadoPago branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,
              action: 'branchRejectOrder',
              platformId: 8,
               order: {
                "id": orderInfo.order.id,
                "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),
                 "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
                "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,
                 "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
              },
              request: {
                 url: url,
                 body: body,
                 header: headersConfig
               }
             });
          }
           break;
        }
       }
 
       case 11: { //'TOQUE'
         const url = `${baseUrlToque}CancelarPedido`;
         try {
          await httpClient.post(url,
            {
              IdPedido: orderInfo.order.id,
              IdMotivo: 21,
              nota: 'Local Cerrado'
            },
            { username: "Toque", password: "Toque" }
          );
         } catch (error) {
          Log.saveError(error, {
            message: 'Platform Communication Error: Toque branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,
            action: 'branchRejectOrder',
            platformId: 11,
             order: {
              "id": orderInfo.order.id,
              "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),
               "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
              "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,
               "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
            },
            request: {
               url: url,
              body: {
                 IdPedido: orderInfo.order.id,
                 IdMotivo: 21,
                 nota: 'Local Cerrado'
               },
               header: { username: "Toque", password: "Toque" }
             }
           });
        }
         break;
       }
 
       case 12: { //'I+D'
         const body = {
// ...
         const headers = {
           'Content-Type': 'application/json'
         };
         const url = `${baseUrlID}CancelarPedido`;
         try {
          await httpClient.post(url, body, headers);
         } catch (error) {
          Log.saveError(error, {
            message: 'Platform Communication Error: I+D branchRejectOrder-C ' + orderInfo.order.id,
            action: 'branchRejectOrder',
            platformId: 12,
             order: {
              "id": orderInfo.order.id,
              "originalId": String(orderInfo.order.id),
               "statusId": orderInfo.order.statusId,
              "branchId": orderInfo.branchId,
               "country": orderInfo.extraData.country
            },
            request: {
               url: url,
               body: body,
               header: headers
             }
           });
        }
         break;
       }
 
       default: //'RESTO'
 
        break;
     }
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { category: 'ORDER', message: '[ORDER/ERROR] reject_order failed', platform: 'Concentrador', internalCode: internalCode, order_id: orderInfo?.id ?? 'unknown' });
   }
 }
