 const login = async (req, res) => {
   try {
     if (!req.body || !req.body.branchId || !req.body.branchSecret) {
       logger.error({ message: 'Insufficient params.', meta: req.body });
       return res
         .status(400)
         .json({
           error: 'Insufficient params.'
// ...
         await syncCheckUTC();
 
 
       }
     } catch (error) {
      Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INFRA', message: '[INFRA/ERROR] cron_already_running failed', platform: 'Concentrador' });
     }
 
     let savedBranch;
     if (req.headers.updaterversion)
       savedBranch = await model
// ...
         aws_id: savedBranch.credentials.aws_id,
         aws_secret: savedBranch.credentials.aws_secret,
         branch_queue: config.AWS.SQS.ORDER_CONSUMER.NAME,
         platform_queue: config.AWS.SQS.ORDER_PRODUCER.NAME.replace('##_', savedBranch.branchId)
       };
       return res
         .status(200)
         .json({
           data: user,
           accessToken
// ...
     return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Login failed.' }).end();
   }
 };
 
 const loginCloud = async (req, res) => {
  let params = {
     user: req.body.user,
    password: req.body.password
   }
   let findParams = {};
   findParams.permissions = true;
  let user = await userModel.findOne({ user: params.user }, { salt: true, password: true });
  if (!user)
    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Username or password are incorrect.' }).end();
  if (user.salt)
    params.password = sha256(params.password + user.salt).toString();
  if (!user.salt) {
     const randomBytes = require('crypto').randomBytes;
     const salt = randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
     const saltedPassword = user.password + salt;
     const password = sha256(saltedPassword).toString();
    await userModel.updateOne({ user: params.user }, { password: password, passwordConfirm: password, salt: salt });
     params.password = sha256(params.password + salt).toString();
   }
  try {
    let user = await userModel.findOne(params, findParams);
     if (!!user) {
      const accessToken = jwt.sign({ user }, settings.token.secret, { expiresIn: '24h' });
       return res.status(200).json({ data: user, accessToken }).end();
    }
     else
      throw 'Username or password are incorrect.';
  } catch (error) {
     Log.saveError(error, { message: 'Falló loginCloud concentrador', platform: 'Concentrador' });
     return res.status(401).json({ error: error }).end();
   }
 };
 
 
 const getBranchesToExport = async (req, res) => {
  try {
     const { chain, country, page, size } = req.body;
     let matchStage = { deletedAt: null };
     if (country?.length) matchStage['address.country'] = { $in: country };
     if (chain?.length) matchStage.chain = { $in: chain };
     const branchesToExport = await model.find(matchStage)
       .select('branchId platforms chain address.country name')
       .sort({ branchId: 1 })
       .skip(page * size)
       .limit(size)
       .lean();
    return res.status(200).json(branchesToExport).end();
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INFRA', message: '[INFRA/ERROR] branches_export failed', platform: 'Concentrador' });
   }
 };
 
 const getBranchesToExportCount = async (req, res) => {
  try {
     const { chain, country } = req.body;
     let matchStage = { deletedAt: null };
     if (country?.length) matchStage['address.country'] = { $in: country };
     if (chain?.length) matchStage.chain = { $in: chain };
    const branchesToExport = await model.countDocuments(matchStage);
    return res.status(200).json(branchesToExport).end();
   } catch (error) {
    Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INFRA', message: '[INFRA/ERROR] branches_export_count failed', platform: 'Concentrador' });
   }
 };

 const getStatus = async (req, res) => {//RESPONDER EN sendOpen
// ...
           const branchName = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == peyaId).branchName;
           const branchNames = Array.isArray(branchName) ? branchName : [branchName];
           for (const remoteId of branchNames) {
             const urlAvailability = `${peyaBaseUrl}/v2/chains/${settings.chainCode}/remoteVendors/${remoteId}/availability`;
             try {
              const statuspos = await httpClient.get(urlAvailability, headersConfig3);
               //retorna el primer estado
               if (statuspos.data[0].availabilityState === "CLOSED") {
                 result.push({ platform: 1, closed: true })
                 continue;
               }
// ...
                 continue;
               }
               throw Error("Estado no existe")
             } catch (error) {
               result.push({ platform: 1, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
              Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INTEGRATION', message: '[INTEGRATION/ERROR] peya_status_check failed', platform: 'PedidosYa-Concentrador', url: urlAvailability, branch_id: branch?.branchId ?? 'unknown', status_code: error?.response?.status ?? 'unknown' });
 
             }
           }
         }
 
// ...
           const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == uberId).branchReference;
           const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
           for (const store_id of branchReferences) {
             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlUber}/v1/delivery/store/${store_id}/status`;
             try {
              if (branch.address.country === "Argentina") {
                let statuspos = await httpClient.get(urlAvailability, headersConfigAr);
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "OFFLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "ONLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: false })
                   continue;
                }
               }
              if (branch.address.country === "Chile") {
                let statuspos = await httpClient.get(urlAvailability, headersConfigCl);
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "OFFLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "ONLINE") {
                   result.push({ platform: 4, closed: false })
                   continue;
                }
               }

               throw Error("Estado no existe")
 
             } catch (error) {
               result.push({ platform: 4, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
              Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INTEGRATION', message: '[INTEGRATION/ERROR] uber_status_check failed', platform: 'UberEats-Concentrador', url: urlAvailability, branch_id: branch?.branchId ?? 'unknown', status_code: error?.response?.status ?? 'unknown' });
             }
           }
         }
 
         // rappi
// ...
           const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == rappiId).branchReference;
           const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
           for (const rappiNumber of branchReferences) {
             if (branch.address.country === "Argentina") {
               try {
                let status = await httpClient.post(urlAr, [rappiNumber], optionsAr);
                 if (status.data[rappiNumber] === false) {
                   result.push({ platform: 2, closed: true })
                   continue;
                 }
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: false })
               } catch (error) {
                 result.push({ platform: 2, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
                Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INTEGRATION', message: '[INTEGRATION/ERROR] rappi_status_check failed', platform: 'Rappi-Concentrador', branch_id: branch?.branchId ?? 'unknown', rappiNumber: rappiNumber, status_code: error?.response?.status ?? 'unknown' });
               }
             }
             if (branch.address.country === "Perú") {
               try {

                 if (status.data[rappiNumber] === false) {

               }

// ...
           const branchReference = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == pedigridoId).branchReference;
           const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
           for (const store_id of branchReferences) {
             const urlAvailability = `${baseUrlPedigrido}v1/locals/horario?branch=${store_id}`;
             try {

             }
           }
         }
 
         // mercadoPago
         if (branchForm.platform === 8) {
           try {
             const platformTemp = branch.platforms.find(pm => pm.platform == mercadopagoId);
             const branchReference = platformTemp?.branchReference;
            const branchReferences = Array.isArray(branchReference) ? branchReference : [branchReference];
            let user_id = platformTemp?.mercadoPagoUserId;
            let tenant = tenants.find(r => r.name === branch.chain.chain.toLowerCase());
             let tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, false);
             let decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
             const headersConfig = {
               headers: {
                 'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
// ...
             for (const store_id of branchReferences) {

               try {
                 let statuspos;
                 try {
                  statuspos = await httpClient.get(urlAvailability, headersConfig);
                 } catch (error) {
                  if (error?.response?.status === 401 && tokenMercadoPagoCloud.tokenCached) {
                    tokenMercadoPagoCloud = await searchToken(user_id, tenant.tenantId, true);
                     decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud = await Token.decrypt(tokenMercadoPagoCloud?.token);
                     const headersConfig2 = {
                       headers: {
                         'Authorization': `Bearer ${decryptedTokenMercadoPagoCloud}`
                       }
                     }
                    statuspos = await httpClient.get(urlAvailability, headersConfig2);
                   }
                   else throw error;
                 }
                 if (statuspos.data.status === "DISABLED" || statuspos.data.status === "PAUSED") {
                   result.push({ platform: 8, closed: true })
// ...
                   result.push({ platform: 8, closed: false })
                   continue;
                 }
                 throw Error("Estado no existe")
               } catch (error) {

                Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INTEGRATION', message: '[INTEGRATION/ERROR] mercadopago_status_check failed', platform: 'MercadoPago-Concentrador', url: urlAvailability, branch_id: branch?.branchId ??                }
             }
           } catch (error) {
             result.push({ platform: 8, closed: "Error: " + error?.message });
            Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INTEGRATION', message: '[INTEGRATION/ERROR] mercadopago_status_check_general failed', platform: 'MercadoPago-Concentrador', branch_id: branch?.branchId ?? 'unknown', order_id: 'unknown', status_code: error?.response?.status ?? 'unknown' });
          }
         }
       } catch (error) {
        Log.saveError(error, { category: 'INFRA', message: '[INFRA/ERROR] get_status failed', platform: 'Concentrador' });
         continue;
       }
     }
     return res.status(200).json(result).end();

     return res.status(200).json(error.message).end();

               },

         }
       }

     strict: false,

Código DESPUÉS

 import mongoose, { Schema } from 'mongoose';
 import uniqueValidator from 'mongoose-unique-validator';
 
 const logErrorSchema = new Schema({
    message:  { type: String, required: true },
    level:    { type: String, enum: ['DEBUG', 'INFO', 'WARN', 'ERROR', 'FATAL'] },
    category: { type: String, enum: ['INTEGRATION', 'ORDER', 'INFRA', 'PERF'] },
    service:  { type: String },
    error:    { type: Object }
 }, {
     strict: false,
     timestamps: true
 });

             } catch (error) {

api/src/utils/httpClient.js

Modificaciones Identificadas

1. Creación del cliente HTTP centralizado

Se define un cliente basado en axios configurando interceptores globales para solicitudes externas.

2. Interceptación y medición de performance

Se calcula la duración de cada petición HTTP y se emite un log automático con categoría de performance.

Código ANTES

Código DESPUÉS

'use strict';
import axios from 'axios';
import Log from './log';

const httpClient = axios.create();

httpClient.interceptors.request.use(config => {
  config._t0 = Date.now();
  return config;
});

httpClient.interceptors.response.use(
  response => {
    const { _t0, _perf = {} } = response.config;
    Log.save('INFO', null, {
      message: `[PERF/INFO] http_response platform=${_perf.platform ?? 'unknown'} action=${_perf.action ?? 'unknown'}`,
      category: 'PERF',
      duration_ms: Date.now() - _t0,
      status: response.status,
      branch_id: _perf.branch_id ?? 'unknown',
      order_id:  _perf.order_id  ?? 'unknown'
    });
    return response;
  },
  error => {
    const config = error.config ?? {};
    const { _t0, _perf = {} } = config;
    if (_t0) {
      Log.save('INFO', null, {
        message: `[PERF/INFO] http_response platform=${_perf.platform ?? 'unknown'} action=${_perf.action ?? 'unknown'}`,
        category: 'PERF',
        duration_ms: Date.now() - _t0,
        status: error.response?.status ?? 'error',
        branch_id: _perf.branch_id ?? 'unknown',
        order_id:  _perf.order_id  ?? 'unknown'
      });
    }
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default httpClient;

Conclusión del Archivo

La nueva utilidad provee un cliente HTTP centralizado que monitoriza tiempos de respuesta y rendimiento operacional.

         }

           action: 'getCloudToken',
           platformId: 8,

             url: urlTokenMercadoPagoCloud,
             method: 'GET',
// ...
       }
       tokenMercadoPagoCloud = tokenMercadoPagoCloud.data;
       // Verificar que la respuesta sea un objeto válido
       if (!tokenMercadoPagoCloud || typeof tokenMercadoPagoCloud !== 'object') {
         Log.saveError(null, {
          message: `C- SmartCloud Error: Invalid response from MercadoPago token endpoint [user_id: ${user_id}]`, 
           action: 'getCloudToken',
           platformId: 8,
           receivedData: tokenMercadoPagoCloud,
           dataType: typeof tokenMercadoPagoCloud,
           platform: 'MercadoPago'       
// ...
         { $set: { tokenMercadoPago: tokenMercadoPagoCloud } },
         { upsert: true, new: true }
       );
       return result.tokenMercadoPago;
     } catch (err) {  
      Log.saveError(err,{message: 'Error getTokenMercadoPagoCloud token from Cloud', user_id , tenantId, url});
       return null;
     }
   }
 
   static async deleteTokens() {
// ...
       // Eliminar tokens que expiran en menos de 24 horas   
       const twentyFourHoursFromNow = new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
       await tokenMercadoPagoModel.deleteMany({ 'tokenMercadoPago.data.expiration': { $lt: twentyFourHoursFromNow } });   
       return 'ok';
     } catch (err) {
      Log.saveError(err, { message: 'Error deleteTokens expired MercadoPago tokens' });
       return null;
     }
   }

 'use strict'
 import logErrorModel from '../models/logError';
 
 class Log {
    static async saveError(error, customData = {}) {
         try {
           // let customDataBefore = { ...customData }; 
            let customDataAfter = { ...customData };
             delete customDataAfter.message;
            try { 
                await logErrorModel.create({
                     message: customData.message,
                    error:{ 
                        error:Log.serializeError(error)  || 'Unknown error',
                         customDataAfter
                     }
                 });
              } catch (ex) {
                if(customData.platform) {
                    await logErrorModel.create({
                        message: customData.message,
                        error: { error: `Error inesperado en ${customData.message}`, platform: customData.platform }
                    });

                } else {
                    await logErrorModel.create({
                        message: customData.message,
                        error: { error: `Error inesperado en ${customData.message}`}
                    });
                }
              }
         } catch (dbError) {
            await logErrorModel.create({
                message: 'Error saveError',
                error: { error: `Error inesperado en saveError`}
             });
         }
     }
    
    // Función para convertir el error a un objeto plano completo
    static serializeError = (err) => {
        if (!(err instanceof Error)) return err;
        return Object.getOwnPropertyNames(err).reduce((acc, key) => {
            acc[key] = err[key];
            return acc;
        }, {});
    };
 }
 
module.exports = Log;

Código DESPUÉS

 'use strict'
 import logErrorModel from '../models/logError';
import sanitizeHeaders from './sanitizeHeaders';

const VALID_LEVELS = ['DEBUG', 'INFO', 'WARN', 'ERROR', 'FATAL'];
const VALID_CATEGORIES = ['INTEGRATION', 'ORDER', 'INFRA', 'PERF'];
const SERVICE_NAME = process.env.SERVICE_NAME ?? 'unknown';
const LOG_STDOUT = process.env.LOG_STDOUT !== 'false';
 
 class Log {
    static _stdout(level, customData, error) {
        if (!LOG_STDOUT) return;
        const { message, category, branch_id, platform_id, order_id, ...rest } = customData;
        const entry = {
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            service: SERVICE_NAME,
            level,
            category: category ?? undefined,
            message,
            ...(branch_id !== undefined && { branch_id }),
            ...(platform_id !== undefined && { platform_id }),
            ...(order_id !== undefined && { order_id }),
            ...(error?.message && { error: error.message }),
            ...(error?.code && { error_code: error.code }),
            ...(Object.keys(rest).length && { rest })
        };
        process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(entry) + '
');
    }

    static async save(level = 'ERROR', error, customData = {}) {
        Log._stdout(level, customData, error);
         try {
            let customDataAfter = sanitizeHeaders({ ...customData });
             delete customDataAfter.message;
            delete customDataAfter.level;
            delete customDataAfter.category;
            try {
                logErrorModel.create({
                     message: customData.message,
                    level: VALID_LEVELS.includes(level) ? level : 'ERROR',
                    category: VALID_CATEGORIES.includes(customData.category) ? customData.category : undefined,
                    service: SERVICE_NAMESS,
                    error: {
                        error: error?.toString() || 'Unknown error',
                        message: error?.message || 'Unknown message',
                        stack: error?.stack || '',
                         customDataAfter
                     }
                 });
            } catch (ex) {
                logErrorModel.create({
                    message: customData.message,
                    level: 'ERROR',
                    service: SERVICE_NAME,
                    error: { error: '[INFRA/ERROR] unexpected_error in Log.save', errormsg: ex.toString() }
                });
            }
         } catch (dbError) {
            logErrorModel.create({
                message: '[INFRA/ERROR] Log.save failed',
                level: 'FATAL',
                service: SERVICE_NAME,
                error: { error: dbError?.toString() }
             });
         }
     }

    static async saveError(error, customData = {}) {
        return Log.save('ERROR', error, customData);
    }
 }
 
\ No newline at end of file
module.exports = Log;

api/src/utils/sanitizeHeaders.js

Modificaciones Identificadas

1. Implementación de sanitizador de secretos

Se crea la utilidad para recorrer objetos de logs de manera recursiva y reemplazar contenidos de Authorization o firmas con la marca REDACTED.

Código ANTES

Código DESPUÉS

'use strict';

function isSensitiveHeaderKey(key) {
    if (!key || typeof key !== 'string') return false;
    const normalized = key.toLowerCase();
    return normalized === 'authorization' ||
        normalized.includes('authorization') ||
        normalized.includes('signature') ||
        normalized.endsWith('sign');
}

function isHeadersContainerKey(key) {
    if (!key || typeof key !== 'string') return false;
    const normalized = key.toLowerCase();
    return normalized === 'headers' ||
        normalized === 'header' ||
        normalized.includes('headers') ||
        normalized.includes('header');
}

function sanitizeHeadersObject(headers) {
    if (!headers || typeof headers !== 'object') return headers;
    if (Array.isArray(headers)) return headers.map(sanitizeHeadersObject);

    const sanitized = {};

    Object.entries(headers).forEach(([key, value]) => {
        if (isSensitiveHeaderKey(key)) {
            sanitized[key] = '[REDACTED]';
        } else if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
            sanitized[key] = sanitizeHeadersObject(value);
        } else {
            sanitized[key] = value;
        }
    });

    return sanitized;
}

function sanitizeHeaders(data) {
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return data;
    if (Array.isArray(data)) return data.map(sanitizeHeaders);

    const sanitized = {};

    Object.entries(data).forEach(([key, value]) => {
        if (isHeadersContainerKey(key) && value && typeof value === 'object') {
            sanitized[key] = sanitizeHeadersObject(value);
        } else if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
            sanitized[key] = sanitizeHeaders(value);
        } else {
            sanitized[key] = value;
        }
    });

    return sanitized;
}

export { sanitizeHeaders };
export default sanitizeHeaders;

Conclusión del Archivo

La nueva utilidad mitiga la exposición accidental de tokens y firmas confidenciales dentro del sistema de logs.

Conclusión del Archivo

Se anexa el diff completo del release como parte de los artefactos de entrega técnico. --- ## Conclusión General del Análisis ## Conclusión General del Análisis Este release del Concentrador representa un avance significativo en la observabilidad y seguridad del sistema. Mediante la centralización de todas las llamadas HTTP externas en un cliente personalizado, el servicio ahora registra y analiza automáticamente los tiempos de respuesta y performance de las integraciones con plataformas clave (PedidosYa, Rappi, UberEats, MercadoPago). Además, la estructuración de trazas de diagnóstico con niveles de severidad y categorías funcionales facilita la integración con agregadores de logs modernos. Por último, la sanitización recursiva de cabeceras sensibles mitiga riesgos de seguridad, previniendo la persistencia accidental de credenciales o firmas en almacenes de datos públicos o internos.

Conclusión General

## Conclusión General del Análisis Este release del Concentrador representa un avance significativo en la observabilidad y seguridad del sistema. Mediante la centralización de todas las llamadas HTTP externas en un cliente personalizado, el servicio ahora registra y analiza automáticamente los tiempos de respuesta y performance de las integraciones con plataformas clave (PedidosYa, Rappi, UberEats, MercadoPago). Además, la estructuración de trazas de diagnóstico con niveles de severidad y categorías funcionales facilita la integración con agregadores de logs modernos. Por último, la sanitización recursiva de cabeceras sensibles mitiga riesgos de seguridad, previniendo la persistencia accidental de credenciales o firmas en almacenes de datos públicos o internos.
